Juya b'ayan da sadiq yayi yasa fatuha jan dan guntun tsaki tare da kallan agogan dake maqale a bango kusan karfe 8:30 na dare, tashi tayi ta bar part din ta koma part din su tana shiga bedroom din ta rage kayan jiki ta tare da shiga toilet ta sheqa waka ta fito daure da towel ko shafa mai ba tayi ba ta bude wardrobe ta Ciro kayan baccin ta riga da wando pink masu Mayan flowers da zanan ya'yan cartoon d'aure gashi k'anta tayi da wata yar kibiya ta d'aure kai sannan ta fesa turara har zata kwanta sadiq ya fado mata a rai ta barshi a d'aki shi ka dai ,gashi baya da lafiya tashi tayi zubur zata fita sai Kuma taci birki tace "Ko naje tijara zai mun dan ba mutunci gare shi ba" karma ya tsinka ni wata zuciyar tace haba fatuha duk rashin mutuncin mutu be lafiya ai dole ya shafa wa mutane lafiya riqe handle din kofar tayi kamar me na zari can kuma ta bude ta wuce part din sadiq  ta shiga dakin har yanzu bacci yake parlour ta d'awo ta kuna kallo dan ba wani bacci take ji ba  cikin jin dadi take kallan arewa24 suna shirin tarkwan kauna  gaba ki d'aya film din ya mata d'adi sosai sai da aka kamala film din misalin karfe 10:00 na dare sannan ta leqa dakin sadiq b'akin bed din ta tsaya tana qaremai kallo a hankali ta kai hannu zata taba goshin shi amma ta k'asa sai dunqwule hannu tayi tana nazarin jikin naji ganin ya motsa ya sata gyara tsayuwa tana kallan yana yin shi lumshe ido tayi a hankali ta kai hannu ta goshin sadiq ji tayi zafi rau kamar garwashi mamaki ne ya kamata da tsoro cikin rawar murya tace "Mai kyau?" shuru sadiq yayi saboda shi ka dai yasan yadda yake jin fever na damu shi kneel down fatuha tayi gaba gadan ta qara cewa mai kyau a qaro na biyu, bude sexy eye's ball's din yayi a hankali yana kallan fatuha data fara matsar kwalla smile yayi yace cikin cool voice nashi "Menene fatuha? kin kasa bacci ne?" girgiza kai tayi alamar a'a to menene kallan shi fatuha ta kumayi tace 'Masasarannan ta matsama baka ji jikin ka da zafi ba da a kauye ne da inna ta dafa ma magunguna kuma da kasha zaka warke amma nan likita sai duba ka yake amma ka qi warkewa mai kyau dan Allah ka daina tunani kaji shiyasa kaqi warkewa ta fadi zancan tana matsar kwalla" kallan ta sadiq kawai yake yana mamaki irin tausayi na fatuha gabaki d'aya tabi ta rude tunda t'aga be lafiya lumshe ido yayi tare da warewa ya sauke so a kanta insha Allah fatuha zan daina kinji murmushi ta saki mai hade da kuka kuka ta tashi ta shiga toilet din sadiq shi kuma ya bita da kallo  ruwa ta debo a babba ball ta tasa towel a ciki ta dawo d'aki har yanzu idanuwan sadiq na kan fatuha ko qif tawa bayayi, sai da ta qaraso gaban shi ya lumshe ido aje ball din tayi a gefen bedside drowe cikin na tsuwa ta shiga matse towel din a hankali ta nufo towel din da goshin sadiq ta fara dadan namai kamar yadda inna ke mata in tana masasara  ko ina na jikin shi ta bi inda ke bude tana danna mai towel din a hankali fever ta fara sauka shi kanshi Sadiq yaji dadin jikin shi sai dai dan dumin da jikin yayi bacci ne ya fara fisgar shi kafin ka ce mi bacci ya kwashe shi, sai fatuha d'ake ta faman dannamai towel.



A hankali ta fara gyan gyadi dan ta fara jin bacci ,sai bacci ya fisgeta sai firgigit ta tashi ta cigaba sai da bacci yaci karfin ta ta fada fadadan qirjin sadiq bacci ya kwashe ta.


      Kiran sallah asuba ne ya tashi sadiq a hankali yake bude sexy eye's ball's din shi ya sauke su kan fatuha da ke bacci malemale a qirjin shi ga gashin ta ya baje saman qirjin shi matsar da gashin yayi yana qarema fuskar ta kallo ,har mamaki wayau irin na fatuha yake gata qarama amma sai dai tana da halin manya yunqurin tashi yayi yaji ta motsa komawa yayi, lumshe ido yayi  lamo kamar me bacci. tashi fatuha tayi tare da ware ido tace "Nashiga uku yaushe bacci ya kwashe ni? wayo Allah na da mai kyau ya tashi ya ganni dana bani" hannu ta kai goshin sadiq jin ba zafi yasata sakin ajiyar zuciya Alhamdulillah jikin yayi sauki bari nayi sauri na d'auro alwala part din ta bari ta wuce nasu.


      Alwala k'awai ta dauro ta gabatar da sallah, bayan ta idar ta tsaya lazimi da bita qaratun ta saboda anjima da rana malam zai amsa .


   Fatuha na fita sadiq ya ware idanuwan shi tare da tabe b'aki yunqurin  tashi yayi yaji hannu shi yayi zafi, sai a lokacin ya tuna da ledar ruwan da aka qaramai har ruwan ya qare ga hannu ya kunbura ya manta shaf be cire ba yaye blanket din jikin shi yayi ya shiga bathroom sai da yayi wanka ya fito sanye da bathrobe da qaramin towel a hannu shi yana goge sumar shi da hannu daya saboda  d'aya hannu namai
zafi  sosai.

    Lokacin yayi dai dai da turowar kofar waigowa yayi yana kallan kofar, fatuha d'ake b'akin kofa itama shi take k'allo kauda kai tayi tare da cewa inakwana amsa mata yayi babu yabo babu falasa qoqarin fita take yace "Fatuha." juyowata tayi ta na kallan sadiq Wanda shi ma ita yake kalla yace "thanks for the caring" murmushi fatuha ta saki duk da bata ji abinda yace karshe ba ta gane mai yake nufi da Kalmar farko   qarasa fita tayi shi kuma ya cigaba da aikin  gaban shi. 

     Yau ma k'amar jiya h'ar yanzu rabi'a b'ata zoba har hankalin fatuha ya fara tashi da rashin  zuwan nata, shara tayi da wanke wanke sannan ta fera dankali ta soya ta juye a kuloli tare da kwai sannan ta daura ruwan zafi ya tafasa ta juye kamar yadda rabi'a keyi haka tayi har su ola sai da ta basu nasu amma har yanzu lamarin rabi'a na bata mamaki zuba k'ayan abincin sadiq tayi a tray ta aje a dining area sannan ta shige bedroom ta cire k'ayan baccin ta shige bathroom tayi wanka tana fitowa mai ta shafa tasa material  red mai ratsin golding a jiki masha Allah fatuha ba qaramin kyau tayi ba daure gashin ta tayi da yar kibiyar soke g'ashi sannan ta fesa turare  mai kamshi sannan ta fito d'aga bedroom,

       Direct dining area ta wuce ta  dau tray din  kayan abinci San an ta wuce part din SadiQ , a hankali ta tura kofar bedroom din kishingide ta ganshi saman bed yana sanye da riga da wando riga mai dowan hannu red mai ratsin black sai jeans blue ba qaramin kyau yayi ba fuskar nan tashi tayi fayau cikin sallama fatuha ta shigo, amsa mata yayi a ta qaice aje tray din tayi a stool, 

      Sannan tace, "Mai kyau ya jiki ?" amsawa yayi cikin cool voice din shi, jiki da sauki fatuha to Allah ya qaro sauki sannan ta zauna saman sofa tana facing din sadiq wayar shi ce d'ake saman bedside drowe ta fara ruri hannu ya kai ya dauka sunan da ya gani jikin screen din wayar ya sasa smile,

   D'aga d'ayan bangaran Anuty bilki tace "Hello baby , ya kake ?" lafiya lau  Maman hanan ya naji voice naka wani iri , yanzu na ta shi d'aga bacci ya fada a taqaice , murmushi tayi d'aga d'ayan bangaran, bari na baka jidda tun jiya  take matsamin tayi missing naka kai da fatuha smile sadiq yayi Wanda ya bayana fararan hakuran shi miqawa angel wayar Anuty bilki tayi, daddy? yes my princesses, dadd I miss u miss u more angel daddy yaushe zaka zo d'auka na? sai end of this week angel yeee daddy  love u dadd ina Anuty fatuha gatanan kusa dani pls dadd ka bata muyi magana I miss her ok Angel lemme give her.

     Fatuha dake zaune saman sofa sai smile take saboda ta gane da jidda sadiq yake waya, saboda da ita ka dai yake waya yana smile ko mom miqa mata wayar yayi ta kara a kunne cike da jin dadi tace Jidda na'am Anuty dan bata fuska fatuha tayi hakan ya  fito mata da sak yarintar ta , jidda haka ake ki tafi ki barni ni ka dai a gida dan Allah ki dawo kin ji, laaa Anuty baga dadd ba ya tayaki wasa ai daddy ya iya wasa dago ido tayi ta kalli sadiq da shima din ita yake kallo ta hadi wani yawu tace cikin zuciyar ta gaskiya jidda ba hankali ina ni ina wasa da sangami mtww Anuty da jidda ta ce ne ya maido da fatuha daga dugwan tunanin da ta tafi sun dade sosai suna waya da jidda har mamaki fatuha take yadda suka dade kamar ba kudi ake dibaba. 

      Miqawa sadiq wayar tayi tare da qoqarin bude kulolin abincin dake saman stool, saving nashi ta shiga yi chips n egg sai farfesun yan ciki dake ta zuba kamshi ni kaina ganin qoqarin fatuha nake dan yanzu baza ta wuce 14 years ba, bayan ta kamala saving din nashi ta miqamai plate din hade da fork amsa yayi yana qoqarin daukar fork din da hannu amma ya qasa saboda hannu yayi tsamin daurewa yayi zaiyi ta maza ya ci da hannu kan dole amma ya kasa fatuha na lura da yadda yake matsawa kan shi taimakamai tayi ta amshi plate din sadiq ya bita da kallo debo chips din tayi ta kai mai baki duk da tana gudun tsinki na sadiq aiko taci za'a ba musu ya amsa haka ta cigaba da feeding din shi yana amsa har sai da chips din ya qare sannan fatuha ta saurara mai, tana qoqarin qaromai wani zaro ido sadiq yayi waje yace fatuha wazai ci wannan? kai mana lalai to wannan din da kyar na dura shima anfada maki kowa irin kine acici harara fatuha ta aika mai ta na qoqarin tashi riqe hannu ta yayi yace sorry murmushi ta saki sannan ta dau plate din chips din tana ci.


      Rayuwa na tafiya komai na gudu kwanakin da sadiq ya dauka yana rashin lafiya duk wata dawainiyar shi fatuha ce take bashi kullawa, hakan yasa wata irin shaquwa ta shiga tsakanin su da sadiq a yanzu jin fatuha yake kamar qanwar shi hakama itama fatuha kusan yanzu kullun suna tare sai dai inya tafi aiki ko masallaci yanzu sosai fatuha ke jin dadin gidan haka shima sadiq duk wani tunani da damuwa ya ragu ba kamar da ba, har wata yar qiba yayi saboda fatuha bata barin shi da yunwa dan yanzu kusan ita ke  feeding din shi duk wani abinci tana bashi tana mai hira har ya cinye be Sani ba    kullun fatuha sai tasasa dariya da labaran ta haka rabi'a ma ta dawo ta amshi aikin ta ashe itama fever tayi.


     D'ariri kun d'ake tashi    a dakin sadiq ,ya Sani tura kofar kar ayi ban dani fatuha ce sanye da rigar sadiq ta uniform na wajan aiki ala dole sadiq take  kwaikwaya dariya Sadiq ya saki  tare da matsowa kusa da ita ja mata hancin yayi tare da cewa "Fatuha baki ji ko?" dariya ta saki tace "Mai kyau ai haka kake yi inkasa uniform ta ida zancan tana qoqarin fita daga bedroom din "riqe hannu ta yayi ya ce " Anjima ki shirya zamu je dauko angel tsale tayi tare da rungume sadiq tana d'ariya shima din rungume ta yayi yana smile dan sakin sadiq tayi tace "Wallahi da jidda b'ata nan gidan ba dadi "ko eh mana ok oya kije ki shirya ki cire min kaya bata fuska sadiq yayi yace 2days dinnan kin fara rai nani Allah, turo b'aki gab'a fatuha tayi tace " Yi hakuri smile Sadiq yayi yace wannan b'akin naki ya iya b'ada hakuri kuma ya iya rashin jin" zame wa tayi d'aga jikin sadiq  ta fita tana saukowa downstairs ta hango baba jinmai na shigowa da k'aya niqi  niqi wani irin ihu ta saki ta ruga da gudu wajan ta ta rungume ta sadiq da ke qoqarin shiga toilet ya fito da sauri ya rife part din shi chak ya tsaya a up stairs yana mamakin rashin hankali na fatuha guntun saki yaja ya juya ,kallo baba jinmai tabi fatuha da shi tana mamakin rigar da ta gani jikin ta dan murmushi tayi Wanda iya Karshi lips din ta, fatuha ta katse mata tunanin da cewa baba shikenan sai ki tafi ki barni  ni ka dai a gida nan? dan Allah yi hakuri fatuha wallahi hidimomine suka min yawa kuma b'ada niyar Dade wa naje ba  niyata nayi kwana biyu na d'awo toh shinkenan bari nayi sauri nayi wanka dauko jidda zamuyi d'aga gidan Anuty bilki  toh baba tace fatuha ta wuce bedroom baba jinmai ta bita da kallan tuhuma Allah ya rufa asiri sannan ta dau ledar tsaraba k'ayan miya ta shige kitchen

Bayan ta tsala wanka ta shafe jikin ta da lotion mai kamshi da sanyi dadi, riga da sket tasa na atamfa mai ratsin pink green blue masha Allah k'ayan sun mata kyau sai suka qara fitowa da haskyan ta  daura dan kwali tayi ya zauna sosai a kanta ta janyo green veil din ta sa  sannan ta sura takalman ta black tare da fesa turare ,b'ayan ta kamala ta fito d'aga bedroom parlour ba kowa sai kamshin girki baba jinmai da ke ta tashi, 

     Part din sadiq ta wuce a hankali ta bude kofar parlour, ba kowa sai sanyi AC d'ake tashi  cikin natsuwa ta nufi bedroom ta burda handle din kofar tsaye ta hango sadiq  gaban dressing mirror sanye da blue riga mai gajeran hannu sai blue Jeans ya gyara sajan shi yayi luf luf haka sumar kanshi ta kwanta yayi kyau sosai ,juyowa yayi su kayi ido hudu da fatuha kashe mata ido yayi yace kallan fa murmushi tayi ta kauda kai tana qoqarin fita yace wallahi kika fita ban zuwa da ke make kafada tayi tare da qarasa shigowa murmushi yayi yace "Good girl" yana qoqarin saka mabalin rigar shi b'ayan ya kamala ya dauki car key din shi da wayan shi dake bedside drowe  ya nufo inda fatuha take yace inyee yan'mata ansha kyau ashe dai yan kauye sun iya gayu, dariya fatuha tayi ta rufe fuskar ta tana aika mai harara oya tashi mu tafi a tare suka fito d'aga part din shi lokaci yayi dai dai da fitowar rabi'a zata tafi gida  kallo tabi so da shi tana mamaki yaushe sadiq ya fara wannan wasan tap, koma dai minene ai fatuha yarinya ce akan yan'matan dake mai layi kumama ko giyar wake yasha abinda take tunani ba shi bane watsar da zancan tayi tare da yin gaba fatuha ce ta kira sunan ta rabi'a waigowa tayi a dan tsorace tare da sakin murmushin da iyakar shi labanta ,har zaki tafi eh wallahi na gama aiki na kuma dama gashi baba jinmai ta dawo toh ki gaida gida gida zai ji fatuha ta Sakai ta fita.

    Suna fitowa k'allo ya koma Kansu ola jiki na bari ya nufo sadiq har qasa ya gaisheshi imeka da Sunday duk sai da suka kwashi gaisuwa, sannan ya bude murfin mota ya shige haka ma fatuha ya ja motar suka bar harabar gidan cikin natsuwa yake driven din waigowa yayi ya kallin fatuha da ta rakufe guri daya yace "Fatuha "na'am yau anfito bude ido ko Dan smile tayi tace tunda nazo birni wannan ce fita tana na biyu ai qara haka  saboda bana san to much of yawa um kina san karatu saurin d'aga kai tayi inna saki school kina so kirinqa karatu kina kulla da angel zaki iya saurin d'aga kai tayi ga farin ciki shinfide a fuskar ta insha Allah zan saki kin ji, lokacin yayi dai dai da  tsayawar motar su bakin geta din gidan Anuty bilki horn yayi mai gadi ya bude masu ya kunna hancin motar shi,


    Dai dai parking space yayi parking tare da kashe motar ,fatuha ta fito shima ya fito yana gaba fatuha bayan shi knocking din kofar sukayi baba larai ce ta bude masu washe da baki har qasa ta gaishe da su sannan ta basu hanya suka shigo cikin sallama Anuty bilki da ke parlour zaune saman three star tana kallo washe da baki ta amsa sannu da zuwa baby  saman two star ya zauna fatuha kuma ta zauna a qasa kusa da sadiq tare da gaishe da Anuty bilki, cikin fara'a ta amsa kwallawa baba larai kira tayi ta kawo masu kayan motsa baki aiko ko 5 minute batayi ba ta d'awo riqe da tray cike da kayan motsa baki ta aje gaban Sadiq ,kallan fatuha yayi dake zaune gefan shi a qasa tayi shuru itama shi din take kalla harara ya aika mata yace tashi ki zauna a kujera Anuty bilki smile tayi tace aiko dai tayi zaune qasa abin ta ni ban ma gane ta ba wallahi naga ta girma sosai raban dana ganta tunda su mom suka tafi yau kusan 2month kenan, ai jiyama mukayi waya dasu mom insha Allah ranar Friday zasu dawo tare da yaya jawad, aini fushi nake da su sun barni ni ka dai a gida  ai sun kyau dama shagwabar ka ta ishemu ya kamata ka zo kayi aure haka naga har wata yar qiba kayi fatuha dake zaune saman one star ce ta kalli sadiq shima ita yake kalla ji tayi gaban ta ya yanke ya fadi da furicin Anuty bilki kauda kai tayi. Kalla ta Anuty bilki tayi tace na aiki su hanan ne amma yanzu kinga sun d'awo bata ida rufe bakin ta ba suka shigo da gudu angel ta fada jikin daddy murmushi Sadiq ya saki daddy I miss u miss u more angel kissing di kumatunta yayi itama tayi mai hanan cikin fara'a ta nufi fatuha basma kuma na gefe tsaye tana satar kallan sadiq kasancewa ta zo Hutu gidan Anuty bilki, gaishe da sadiq sukayi ya amsa a takaice sannan hanan taja fatuha suka wuce dakin ta  angel kuma na maqale da daddy 

      Suna shiga d'akin hanan basma ta cire mayafin kanta ta fada saman bed,  itama hanan bed ta hau fatuha kuma ta  zauna saman sofa, laa fatuha baki zama saman bed a'a bari a nan ma yayi wallahi basma kinga wadda nake fada maki mai gashin nan d'ago kai tayi ta kalli fatuha ta dan yi smile sannu ki fa yauwa inji fatuha hira suka shiga zubawa kasan Cewar duk sa'anin junane amma dai basma ta dan girme su dan yanzu ita SS2 zata hanan kuma SS1 kunsan a tare suke komai da hanan da basma duk da basma Anuty ce a wajan hanan sosai fatuha ta saki jikin ta amma sai dai tana dan dari dari da basma , kiran sallah magrib ne ya tashe su d'aga fira alwala suka dauro suka gabatar da sallah bayan sun idar angel ta shigo da gudu d'akin tace "Anuty fatuha daddy yace ki fito mu tafi?" to tace tare da yafa mayafin ta a tare suka fito d'asu hanan parlour a tsaye suka tada sadiq Anuty bilki ce ta bata rai tace haba baby kabari kuyi dinner mana sai ku tafi a'a gaskiya yau girkin baba jinmai zanci I miss her food ohni ba kayi missing din nawa ba ko ba haka bane sis so kike muyi barnan na gida ko toh shikenan a gaida gida dan ko rakiyar banyi langwabe kai sadiq yayi pls kar muyi haka mana harara wasa ta kaima sannan ta ja veil din ta dake saman kujera suka fito a tare hira suke sosai kamar karsu bari , bude murfin mota sadiq yayi angel ta shige gaba fatuha na b'aya sannan ya ja motar  be zame dasu ko ina ba sai shop right shiga su kayi yayi masu siyaya sosai ice cream teddy's  duk ya kwaso kayan wasa kamar be jin kudin ya cika boot da su sannan suka juyo gidan a gajiye likis.

       Yana horn bala mai gadi ya bude mai geta yayi parking sannan yayi masu ola nuni da su kwaso kayan boot lokaci ma jidda tayi bacci, fatuha ce ta dauko ta suka shige gida  upstairs sadiq ya haye fatuha ta shige bedroom kaya kawai ta cire ma jidda tasa mata na bacci, tana qoqarin cire nata akayi knocking din kofar zura hijabi tayi ta bude ola ne riqe da trolley din jidda da k'ayan wasan ta duk ya aje sannan ya mata sai da safe rufe kofar tayi ta rage kayan jikin ta ta wuce toilet tayi wanka ta fito daure da towel tsane jikin ta ta yi ta shafa mai sannan tasa rigar baccin ta gown dai dai gwiya white n brown mai zanan cartoons sannan ta saki gashin kanta tare da daukar jidda part din sadiq ta wuce da ita a parlour ta same shi yana k'allan news ,sanye da k'ayan baccin shi maroon riga da wando mai dogwan hannu bedroom ta shige ta shinfidar da jidda sannan ta fito har yadda ta barshi a parlour har yanzu haka yake qoqarin fita take yace fatuha ki kawo min dinner nan to ta ce mai sannan ta fita.

    Direct dining area ta wuce ta zubo mai tuwan shinkafa miryar taushe tasha naman rago sai kamshi ke tashi ,sannan ta wuce  part nashi saman stool ta aje mai plate din zata fice riqo hannu ta Sadiq yayi tare da cewa "Fatuha, waya bata makin rai tunda muka dawo naga baki farin ciki? ko bros ya maki laifine?" laa Allah ba abinda kamin kawai na gajine yau munsha hira da su hanan toh naji amma ai ba kiyi dinner ba bana jin yunwa ice cream din dana sha ya cika min ciki wallahi  ok tunda hakane na fasa kai ki school din zaro ido waje fatuha tayi dan Allah kayi hakuri Allah zanci tam oya zo zauna muci zame hannu ta tayi daga ruqwan da sadiq ya mata ta zauna a qasa tare da janyo plate din gaban ta shima sadiq din zama qasa yayi cikin natsuwa ta fara kai loma daya sannan ta debo ta kaima sadiq amsa yayi wata lomar ta debo ta kai mai baki hadawa yayi da yatsanta ya ciza yar qarasa ta saki tana yar fe hannu ta dariya sadiq ya saki ,kuka ta fara mai tana kaimai bugun wasa riqe hannu yayi yana dariya ya rungume ta sorry sis yana magana yana shafa bayan ta lamo tayi a fadandan qirjin sa tana maida numfashi dago da fuskar ta yayi yace sis yau baki surutu kamar ankule maki baki gashi kin bata min night dress da miya gaskiya ba zan yadda ba ya fadi a dan shagwabe, kallan shi fatuha tayi ta lumshe ido tare da cewa yi hakuri mai kyau bazan sake ba good girl to fada min Fushin nan name to um um dama ina so naje kauye ne kwana biyu wajan inna kafin na fara zuwa makaranta wannan shine damuwar eh ok karki damu insha Allah zan kai ki zame jikin ta fatuha tayi daga na sadiq riqe habar ta Sadiq yayi yace fatuha ki dauke ni kamar yayan ki duk abinda ke damun ki ki fada Mani na maki alqawarin samamaki farin ciki kamar yadda kika samamin fatuha murmushi ta saki wanda ya bayana fararan hakuran ta daukar plate din abinci tayi zata fita sadiq ya rako ta har kitchen ta aje plate din ta wanke hannu ta.


      sai da ta zo b'akin kofar Shiga bedroom sadiq ya mata pack a goshi itama ta mashi a kumatu sannan ta shige bedroom shima ya haura upstairs. 


     B'ayan kwana biyu h'aka rayuwa ta cigaba da tafiya inda sadiq ke b'awa fatuha da jidda kulawa sosai haka suma suna faran tamai rai duk wanda yaga sadiq a yanzu yasan b'aya fama da wata matsala ,sai wani kyau da yake ta qarawa bangaran karatun adini kuwa fatuha tayi nisa sosai tana mai da hankali yadda ya kamata  kuma a yanzu duk duniya bata da aboki ko qawa sama da captain shine uwar ta shine uban  b'ata bari yayi fushi da ita ko kadan baba jinmai ko duk yadda ta bigi cikin fatuha ta fada mata wani abu na daban sai ta gane abinda take tunanin ba haka bane zala shaquwace kawai tsakin sadiq da fatuha. 

     
   Dr yakub yafi kowa farinciki da canji irin na sadiq har mamaki yake da hamdala da sadiq yaji shawarar shi. 

      Kwana biyu ko d'aya je wajan ammi ma taji d'adi ganin canjin shi h'aka majid da kwana shi biyu da dawowa d'aga Lego's anmai transfer ya d'awo kano da aikin sosai sadiq yaji d'adi hak'a ko ba komai dan uwan shi ya d'awo kusa da shi. 

    
     Kasancewa yau ta k'ama friday shiye shiye ake sosai na tarbar su mom ,yau zasu d'awo Nigeria tare da yaya jawad h'aka Anuty bilki  ta kwaso duka yaranta tazo dasu  anata aiki , fatuha na h'ango sanye da uniform din ta na yan'aiki wanda ta Dade raban data sasu sun mata kyau sosai part din sadiq ta wuce riqe da cup ganin b'aya parlour yasa ta tura qofar d'akin yana tsaye gaban dressing mirror sanye da shada blue ta sha aiki da dark blue zare yana combing din gashi kan shi, murmushi ya saki tare da qarasowa wajan fatuha amshe cup din yayi ya aje saman stool kallo tabi shi da shi dan yau ba qaramin kyau ya mata ba har mamakin kanta take har yau ta kasa ganin wanda yafi sadiq kyau da komai gashi kullun qara kyau yake ,hura mata iskar da akayi ya sata ware manyan idanuwan ta tana kallan sadiq, smile yayi yace "Tunanin fa? kin hanani tunani amma ke gashi kinayi yanzu" ah bafa tunani nake ba to mikikeyi bayan gashi na gani um kayi kyau thanks  ke  ma kinyi kyau a haka din eh mana cup din ya dauka ya kai bakin shi hmmm ya lumshe ido lalai fatuha ta iya hada coffee gaskiya zani  da ke dariya tayi tace "Allah idan na biye ma sai mu kwana anan muna surutu hehe" ko tam shikenan tafi tunda tafiya zakiyi ni bana so kiyi aikin shiyasa nace akiramin ke amma tunda kin fi so ki wahala shikenan tafi munma bata, langwabe kai fatuha tayi tace "Kayi hakuri ka ga su mom ne zasu d'awo kaji" ok  amma sai kin min pack ko kina tafiya to tace tare da yimai pack a goshi sannan ta fice d'aga d'akin sadiq ya bita da k'allo.

Da misalin karfe 1:00pm flight din su mom ya sauka Nigeria lokacin har Sadiq ya Isa airport da yaran shi ,kusan mota uku akayi ta tarbar su mom da abba a hankali suke saukowa d'aga matatakalar jirgin mom na sanye da lace ash mai ratsin pink blue abba na sanye da shada brown tasha aiki da dark brown zare yaya jawad da Anuty shukura na b'aya suma sunsha shigar alfarma tana riqe da baby ta , jawahir yana riqe da hannu baban shi cikin fara'a suka qaraso gaban motocin.

     Cikin sasarfa sadiq ya qarasa ya kai ma mom hug, dan murmushi ta saki baby wai yau she zaka girma ne? inji abba dake gefe mom b'ata fuska sadiq yayi irin na shagwaba tare da cewa "Danma banyi kuka ba kun tafi kun barni yar dariya jawad yayi sannan yaran sadiq suka bude masu motocin suka shige .

    
   A tare motocin suka kunno kai gida bala mai gadi ya wage geta washe da baki yana daga masu hannu, dai-dai parking space motocin suka faka suka fara fitowa tun k'afin su shigo gida yan gida suka fito kowa ka kalla kasan cike yake da farin ciki ,haka jikokin a tare suka shige parlour su ola suka shige da k'ayan su ciki.

    Murna wajan sadiq ba'a magana saboda d'ama yayi missing din mom din shi dakuna suka shige matafiyan suka sheqa wanka, sai misalin karfe 3:00pm aka halara a dining area duk wanda ka kalla cikin farin ciki yake cikin natsuwa suke lunch din inda jawad da sadiq suke ta zuba hira Anuty bilki da shukura matar jawad amma duk wannan hira sadiq na kalle kalle ya ga ta inda fatuha zata fito, abinci ne gaban shi amma ya qasa ci saboda yanzu ya saba tare suke ci da fatuha ko tayi feeding din shi loma biyu zuwa uku yayi ya ture abincin.

    Bangaran fatuha ko tunda ta kamala aiki cikin ta ke nukurkusa duke take a daki sai kuka take zubarwa, qoqarin miqewa take ta kasa saboda cikin ba qaramin kullewa yayi ba bude kofar da a kayi yasa fatuha d'ago jajayan idanuwan ta hanan ce tsaye bakin kofar cikin sauri ta qaraso fatuha meke damunki ta fadi a dan tsura ce kuka ta kuma saki tana riqe da marata, ganin ba abinda fatuha take cewa yasa hanan fita lokaci kowa na parlour ana zuba hira Momi tace cikin rawar murya kowa ya waigo Momi fatuha ba lafiya gata can kwance tana kuka wai cikin ta mikewa tsaye sadiq yayi har suna yar rige rige da Anuty bilki h'aka mom ta biyu bayan su suna Shiga suma a durkushe suka same ta ,sai kuka da take ta fama rairawa Anuty bilki ta matso kusa da ita tare da cewa "Fatuha minene ?" cikin kuka kuka tace "Ciki na Momi " sorry tashi a kaiki asibiti kinji bana iya tashi ta fadi tana qara sakin saban kuka Anuty bilki ce ta taimaka mata ta tashi gabaki daya sket din ta ya baci da jini ashe aladace ta fara,   sannu kin ji kallan mom Anuty bilki tayi ta mata nunu da ta kori su sadiq waje aiko haka akayi aka tasa keyar su waje.

    
      Sannu kinji fatuha girmane ya zo kinji ba wani abu bane, yanzu Shiga toilet ki wanke jikin ki sai kin fito na gwada maki yadda ake d'aga kai fatuha tayi ta shige toilet a duduke Anuty bilki ta fita taba sadiq kudi ya siyo pant ya d'awo kallo ya bita da shi tare da cewa ki bar kudin bari naje na siyo, murmushi tayi ta maida kudin ta cikin handbag sannan sadiq ya fita ko minti 10 beyi ba ya d'awo ya miqa mata part din, ta qara komawa lokacin fatuha ta fito duk yadda ake anfani da part haka Anuty bilki ta gwada ma fatuha tare da bata yan shawarwari na gyara jikin ta saboda yanzu ita ba yarin ya bace sosai ta ji dadin sannan ta bata magani  tasha saman bed ta koma ta kwanta bacci ya sure ta Anuty bilki ta fita. 

    Basu suka bar parlour ba sai da akayi kiran sallah magrib yasa suka wuce masallaci ,su kuma matan suka wuce part din mom basu suka dawo ba sai bayan isha'i jawad ya wuce part din su sadiq ya shige gida parlour ba kowa bedroom din fatuha ya wuce a hankali ya bude kofar kwance ya hango ta saman bed ta dunqule waje daya rife kofar  yayi tare da qarasa shigowa gaban bed din yayi kneel down  a hankali ya kai hannu yana shafa mata gashin kanta a hankali ta ware idanuwan ta kan sadiq, kuka ta saka mai mai cike da shagwaba da yarin ta, "shhh kukan ya Isa haka mana um kamo hannu shi tayi tana qoqarin kai mai inda ke mata ciwo sauri kauda hannu yayi tare da wayin cewa dan ya lura har yanzu fatuha yarin yace shiyasa take haka mai kyau na'am Sister dai ciwo eh mana gobe da kin tashi zaki ji kin warke kin ji sis to ta ce mai tare da riqe hannu shi tana wasa da su kamar an tsunkune shi ya tashi ya fita fatuha ta bishi da kallo.

    

     Kitchen ya shige ya d'auko mata fresh milk mai sanyi ya d'awo bedroom din har yanzu tana yadda ya bar ta  bata fresh milk din yayi ta Kafa kai ta shaye sannan ya mata pack tare da yi mata good night ya fice.



   Kun san kwanan fatuha 4 tana period  tayi wanka mom ta gwada mata duk yadda ake  h'aka rayuwa ta cigaba da tafiya cikin jin dadi inda yanzu duk gidan ba wanda bai san shaquwar fatuha da sadiq ba, mom ko in ban da dadi ba abinda take ji dan gani take fatuha alheri ce a rayuwar su sannan sadiq ya sheda mata zai kai fatuha school kuma tayi na'am da zancan.

       Harhada k'ayan ta d'ana jidda take a trolley saboda yau zasu je wudil, baki har kunne b'ayan ta gama hada kayan ta wuce part din sadiq zaune a parlour ta same shi harara ya aika mata tare da kauda kai, dan bata fuska fatuha tayi tare da matsawa kusa da sadiq mai kyau! qara kauda kai yayi, yi hakuri innama laifi ta mike zata fita ruqo ta yayi tare da  wace shine dan zaki tafi gida shine ko ki leqo da safe yi hakuri, ka hada kayan naka ?nop to na hada ma eh yace a taqaice aiko d'akin ta shige ta h'ada mai nashi k'ayan cikin trolley ta fito da su d'ama shima ya shirya saboda tsabar zumudi ko break ta qasayi  haka mom ta mata shatara na arziki har bakin mota ta rako su ace warsu kwana biyu zasuyi su dawo saboda ankusa komawa  school da sun dawo fatuha zata fara zuwa.

     Sannan sadiq ya ja mota suka bar harabar gidan angel sai murna take tare da zuba masu surutu har bacci ya kwashe ta.  



       Ko 2 hours basuyi ba suka Isa wudil tunda suka iso fatuha ke bin kauyan da kallo ,rayuwar ta da fatsima ta d'awo mata sabuwa  dai dai kofar gidan su fatuha akayi parking har yanzu gidan na yadda yake sai dai dan tsufa da ya fara sai yar kofar da akasa cikin sauri ta shige gidan, 

     Inna na zaune saman kujera tayani gulma tana tankade, fatuha ta shigo da gudu ta fada jikin ta dagowa inna tayi a dan  tsorace washe baki tayi ganin fatuha binta inna tayi da ido dan dai fatuha na diyar ta da tace ba diyar ta bace saboda girma da haske da ta qara sallamar sadiq ce ta katse mata tunani, kinga inna na bar mai kyau waje shida jidda fita tayi tare da ba sadiq hakuri ta ruqo jidda suka shigo har qasa sadiq ya gaishe da inna, fatuha tayi ma inna bayani aiko inna hannu biyu biyu ta amshe su dan ta yaba da hankalin sadiq jidda gani tayi ta samu kaka ta maqalewa inna da gudu fatuha fita daga gidan sadiq ya fita da ido bata zame ko ina ba sai gidan su fatsima tana Shiga ta shiga kwalla mata kira ba fatsima ba har gwago a rude suka fito saboda jin muryar da suka dade suna kewa, da gudu fatsima ta fada cikin fatuha tare da sakin kukan farin cikin murna ba'a magana d'akin ta ja fatuha sai dai yanzu fatsima duk tayi sanyi nan take taba fatuha labari ranar daurin auranta mijin ya rasu fatuha sai da ta tausaya mata sannan suka koma gida a tare lokacin inna har ta bama Sadiq masauki tare da kayan motsa baki jidda kuma na maqale da ita. 

   Kun san a tare suka wani da fatsima inda sadiq ke ta fushi fatuha ta barshi shi d'aya, basu suka rabu da fatsima ba sai gab da magrib lokacin baffa ya dawo shima cike da farin cikin inda ya dinga jan sadiq a jiki suna  taba hira Sama sama. inda inna ta cika mai fura da nono da tuwan dawa miyar kuka tasha manshanu sosai sadiq ya saki jiki yaci abincin.

    
     Misalin karfe 8:30pm b'ayan sun d'awo d'aga masallaci sadiq ya wuce masaukin shi, b'ayan fatuha ta gama sa k'ayan bacci ta shirya jidda itama cikin nata sannan ta h'ada ma sadiq coffee kamar yadda ta saba a gida ta ja hannu angel suka wuce masaukin cikin sallama ta d'aga labilan d'akin suka shigo, amsawa yayi a taqaice ta aje mai coffee ya amsa tare da aika mata harara wasa hannuwanta biyu tasa a kunne alamar bada hakuri hira sukayi sama sama inda angel ke ta zuba qiriniya har bacci ya kwashe ta daukar ta fatuha tayi tana qoqarin fita sadiq ya masu pack a tare suka wuce d'akin fatuha. 


     *Washe gari*

Tunda safe fatuha ta tashi ta share tsakar gida tasses, ta hura icce ta dora koko tare da dumama tuwan jiya, ta juye a kwanan samira tana qoqarin fitowa d'aga kitchen din inna ta fito d'aga uwar daka washe da baki tana bin tsakar gida da kallo , alalai fatuha anyi hankali har qasa ta gaida inna ta amsa cikin fara'a sannan ta shige qurya ta fido jidda ta wanke ta tas ta shirya ta cikin jean n t-shirts masu kyau sannan itama ta shige kewaye tayi wanka, b'ayan ta fito ta shirya cikin riga da sket na lace red mai kyau sai yar huda d'ata murza da lipstick ba qaramin kyau tayi ba sannan ta dau kwanonin da tasama sadiq abinci ta nufi d'akin inna da ke surfa gero tace "Aiko dazu da sasafe tare suka fita da baffa ki gona" murmushi fatuha ta saki tace shine baffa beje dani ba ko? ta zuburo b'aki tare da shigewa ciki ta aje mai abincin tana fitowa ta ci qaro da fatsima washe da baki ta tarbeta kusan a tare su kayi kallaci sannan suka fita yawo ganin gari duk inda fatuha ta wuce sai antanka da irin kyanda tayi da natsuwar da ta qara haka aka rinqa Layin ganin ta inda ta rinqa baje tsaraba tana rabawa fatsima ko tasha tsaraba kamar ba gobe.


     Kwana su biyu a kauye inda yan kauye ke cewa fatuha ta zo da larabawa sun sha yawo da sadiq har rafi sai da ta kai shi, da gona mai gari inda suke satar mangwaro a da, sosai ya ji d'adi kauyan ba dan k'adan ba,


      Fatuha ce riqe da trolley tana rusa kuka ita bata komawa, sai da inna tayi da gaske sannan ta shige mota sadiq yasa trolley a boot tare da yima baffa shatara na arziqi  baffa duk yadda baffa yaqi amsa haka sadiq ya matsa mai amsa tare da samai albarka fatsima har da yar kwallata sannan suka dau hanyar kano.

  Akwana atashi ba wuya wajan sallah fatuha dai ta fara zuwa makaranta inda aka sata jss1 sosai take maida hankali kuma tana gane karatun gashi Allah ya zuba mata qoqari  na ban mamaki, dan har tafi masu qoqarin ajin a yanzu a tare suke zuwa da jidda a yanzu jidda na primary 2 kusan duk makarantar su d'aya da su hanan da basma hakan ya qarasa suka shaqu duk inda kaga hanan to zakaga fatuha ko basma sosai suke maida hankali akan karatunsu.

     K'amar yadda aka saba tashin su misalin karfe 3:30pm driven yayi parking suka bale murfi motar suka fito a tare, sanye fatuha take da uniform din ta riga mai dagwan hannu milk sai sket  dark green dai -dai gwiwa sai dan qaramin hijab milk jidda kuma biri da wando milk a ciki rigar saman green kallo d'aya zaka masu kasan sun gaji likis tun a parlour jidda ta fara wurgi da jakar ta, ohno why this angel bari dai grandma ta fito ta Zane ki, sama nayi da ido ina tunanin wake wurgo wannan turancin kamar a mafarki ashe fatuha ce ohni salma😧, daukar bag din jidda tayi tana zunburo baki ta kai bedroom din ta, 

    Har fatuha ta shiga bedroom din ta ta fara cire mabalin rigar ta, ta tuna da ice cream din ta d'ake bedroom din sadiq cikin fridge da sauri ta fito ta haura upstairs a h'ankali ta bude part din ta shige  parlour ba kowa sai kamshi d'ake tashi, bedroom ta shige tare da nufar fridge din ganin Sadiq tsaye yana qoqarin cire rigar uniform din shi tace uncle waigowa yayi cikin smile yes dear um, ya naga ka d'awo yau da wuri haka uncle? na gama  aiki ne gobe da safe flight dina na zuwa  london ze tashi zani wani course ne ba zata ya zo min h'ada kayana na d'awo yi  , dan bata fuska tayi tare nufar fridge ta dau ice cream tana ta kunbure kunbure ta fice Sadiq ya bita da kallo.

              *69_70*

*_👏🏽Godiya gare ku mosayan ❤wannan novell din, ina jin d'adin y'adda kuk'e comments😍 ana mugun tare irin sosai din nan._*🤸🏽‍♀🤸🏽‍♀


*_wannan shafin naki ne Maimuna Lawal Allah ya bar zuminci har ya'ya da jikoki Ameen🤝._*


*F* atuha na shig'a bedroom din ta, ta aje ice cream din saman bedside drowe d'an gabaki d'aya ice cream din yafita ran ta, a duniya ba abinda ta tsana kamar mai kyau yayi nisa da ita, ita kanta har mamakin h'akan take jiki ba qwari ta ida cire school uniform din ta tare da shigewa bathroom ta sheqa wanka ta fito, b'ayan ta tsane jikin ta tabi da lotion mai kamshi tare da sanya black after dress din ta mai Stone's d'aure gashin kanta tayi kamar ganmo tare da Murza yar hod'a  da lipstick masha Allah fatuha ba qaramin kyau tayi ba, fitowa tayi d'aga bedroom din ta wuce na mom cikin sallama ta shiga mom Na tsaye gaban dress Mirror tana shirin da alama fita za tayi cikin fara'a ta amsa,tace "Mom fita zakiyi ne?", eh wallahi zani gidan ammi ne, d'ama yanzu nake cewa ko zaki raka ni ?dan murmushi fatuha tayi itama tana san zuwa wajan ammi din nan amma tunda ta zo sau biyu ta taba zuwa g'ashi tana san ganin basma, amma Assignment ba zai bari ba gashi malam anjima zaizo , mom kin manta yau Wednesday, oh haka fa na manta fa shafa next time munje kuma wallahi mom ina san zuwa basma har ce min take bani da kirki bana zuwa, ai ta fada duk zaryar da suke maki b'aki zuwa masu kema kin fara k'oyan h'alin uncle din naki, dan smile fatuha ta saki mai cike da jin d'adi ita dai duk abinda za'a ce d'aga mai kyau yake so take ,mom ai baya san yawo uncle shiyasa. ke kuma sai  biye mai kike ko? zuminci fa abin wasa bane  mom ta ida zancan tana daukar handbag din ta kusan a tare suka fito, angel na zaune a parlour tana k'allan cartoon ganin grandma zata fita yasa ta tashi grandma zan biki please nop  yau malam zaizo zuburo b'aki tayi gab'a tana sanyin kuka please grandma ki je dani,angel kiyi h'akuri grandma ta je kin ji? muma ranar Friday mun tafi kinji? d'aga kai jidda tayi b'adan taso, ba har b'akin mota suka raka ta suka juyo.

    A dai-dai b'akin kofa suka ci qaro da sadiq sanye da 3 quarter black da white t-shirts da alama gym zashi, k'auda kai fatuha ta yi ala dole fushi take da sadiq, smile yayi tare da d'aga angel sama yana mata wasa tana d'ariya ko k'allan inda fatuha take beyi ba saboda ya lura yau rigima take ji, rabawa tayi ta gefan shi zata wuce ta cika tap qiris take jira ta fashe, ruqo hannu ta yayi h'akan yasa ta tsaya chak a wajan, ta kasa kwace hannu ta sai binshi da take da kallo, aje jidda yayi qasa yana aje ta kamar jira take tayi cikin gida da gudu, fisko fatuha yayi ta fado fafadan qirjin shi har tana jin bugun zuciyar shi lamo tayi a qirjin nashi, a hankali ya d'ago da habar ta yana kallan ta lumshe ido yayi tare da ware su kan fatuha cikin cool voice din shi yace "Fushin na menene?" um dan b'ata rai tayi tana qoqarin zamewa d'aga jikin shi tunda be san fushin me take ba, dan matse ta yayi da karfi ta saki yar qara, ta fara matsar kwalla, d'aga nace zanyi tafiya sai fushi fatuha 3 days fa kawai zanyi kin ji dear, turo b'aki tayi gaba ni ba fushi nake ba. to me kike? ba komai, ok tunda ba fushi kike ba please ki h'ada min k'ayana na rasa k'ayan da zansaka ki riga kin shagwaba uncle da yawa, dan zunburo b'aki ta kuma yi maicike da shagwaba, please karki ce bakiyi, zanyi mana promise ,promise good girl sannan ya mata pack a goshi ya fita.

    B'ayan ta kamala h'ada mai k'ayan sannan ta kalli agogon d'ake mane a d'aki kusan karfe 4:30 sauri fitowa tayi d'aga part din ta wuce bedroom, d'auro alwala tayi tare da gabatar da sallah tana gamawa ta d'auko alQur'anin ta  saboda yau laraba, alqur'ani kawai suke yi tare da janyo hijab din jidda tun a parlour ta zura mata suka fita a tare, lokacin har malam ya zo karatu suka yi sosai INSA malam y'a masu  qarin bayin akan hadisi, sai  gab da magrib suka shigo cikin gida, alwala k'awai suka gabatar tare da yin sallah b'ayan ta idar ta tsaya yin lazimi sannan tayima jidda assignment tayi nata kiran sallah isha ne ya tashe su bayan sun gabatar suka fito parlour sukayi dinner sannan kowa ya wuce bedroom din shi saboda sun gaji likis.


      *Asuba ta gari*

Sallah asuba fatuha tayi tare da yin wanka, mai kawai ta shafa tare da sa yar gown iya karta gwiwa sannan ta fito bedroom din jidda ta shiga tayi mata wanka tare da shirya ta cikin unform sannan ta bar d'akin, kitchen ta shiga cikin sallama baba jinmai nata jefa bafanke gaishe ta fatuha tayi cikin fara'a ta amsa sannan suka gaisa da rabi'a, bude fridge tayi tare da fido yan cikin rago ta gyara ta d'aura kan wuta tare da zama tana taya su baba jinmai aiki ,sai hanata suke amma taqi sai da farfesun yayi ta juye a kola tare da h'ada coffee tasa a tray direct part din sadiq ta wuce ,a hankali ta murda k'ofar parlour ba kowa aje tray din tayi saman stool ta nufi bedroom din tsaye ta h'ango sadiq yana qoqarin sa necktie  sallama tayi tare da gaisheshi cikin fara'a ya amsa sannan ta qaraso ta amshi  necktie din tana samai bayan ta samai ta kalle shi uncle u looking so handsome tare da mai pack shima pack ya mata tare da daukar suit din shi black ya riqe a hannu ta ,janyo trolley din tayi suka fito parlour lokacin angel ta turo kofar tare da cewa "Daddy ta fada jikin sadiq" hug nata yayi d'an shagwabe fuska angel tayi daddy am going to miss u daddy, me to angel, amma daddy zaka min tsaraba teddys yes angel washe b'aki tayi tace "Anuty fatuha k'ema ki fadama daddy tsarabar da zaimaki" smile tayi tare da cewa "Duk abinda daddy yake so shi nake so yamin tsaraba" kallan ta sadiq yake me cike da so sannan ya zauna saman one star tray ta janyo ta zuba mai farfesun a ball saboda sadiq mutun ne mai san farfesu shiyasa fatuha kullun sai ta mai dan ta faran ta mai, feeding din shi farfesun ta fara yana sha angel itama tana bashi haka suka rinqa feeding din juna su cikin jin d'adi happy family sannan suka fito tray din fatuha ta kai kitchen ta wuce bedroom tasa uniform tare da d'auko school bag din ta.

  Fitowar ta parlour yayi dai dai da fitowar su mom da abba cikin natsuwa ta gaishe su pack sadiq ya masu shida angel itama fatuha pack tayi ma mom suka fice a tare mota d'aya suka shiga da sadiq driver na ja sai da aka zo school din su yayi parking, gaba ki daya idanunta sukayi rau rau alamun zatayi kuka, suka bama sadiq hug suka fice yana kallan su har suka shige classes sannan driver ya ja mota sai airport.

    
      Tunda sadiq ya tafi su angel suke missing nashi mom har mamaki suke, bata ko yar rahanan sun daina inkaga suna raha to sadiq ne ya kira waya haka shima can cike yake da kewar su inda yake jin kamar wata yayi ba kwana uku ba.

    Akwana a tashi ba wuya wajan Allah yau kwana sadiq biyar kuma yaune ranar dawowar shi, zo ku ga murna wajan su fatuha sai zuba rawar kai ake mom sai janta take dan yanzu fatuha ta tashi d'aga yar aiki dan tuni sadiq ya yan'tata duk wani abinci da sadiq ke so fatuha ita da kanta ta mai sannan ta jera a dining area.

     Bedroom ta shige tare da yin wanka, k'aya ta fido  ta rasa wanda zata sa duk wanda ta fido sai ta maida can ta h'ango wata red gown me Stone's, aje ta tayi a gefe ta tsantsara kawalliya ba qaramin kyau tayi ba sannan ta zura riga masha Allah indai maganar kyau ake fatuha ta kai, angel ce ta shigo da gudu itama sanye da gown pink tace "Anuty daddy ya dawo?" waigowa tayi tare da yin fari da ido suka fito a tare  shigowar sadiq parlour kenan su fatuha suka fito, k'allo sadiq ya bisu da shi mai cike da so rugawa su kayi a tare suka rungume sadiq d'ariya ya saki zaku ka dani ya fadi mom dake saukowa d'aga upstairs dariya ta saki tace "Sadiq kai da yaran ka kuna dirama wallahi" smile yayi tare da hug din mom in taqaice maku duk wani girki da fatuha tayi sai da sadiq ya ci inda yake ta binta da k'allo.

      B'ayan ya hutu ya fido tsaraba yabama fatuha I phone 6 waya dadi da murna wajan fatuha ba'a magana jidda kuma Tap Apple mai shegyan kyau tare da new Teddy's h'aka suka dinqa zuba murna baki har kunne.


*2years letter*

Wata kyakyawar matashiyar budurwace da baza ta wuce 16 years ba ta rugu da gudu d'aga upstairs gudu take amma komai na jikin ta amsawa yake sanye take da 3 quarter pink sai riga white mai gajeran hannu ban gaje mutun da tayi ne ya sata tsayawa chak, cikin cool voice din ta tace "Sorry tare da rabawa ta gefan shi ta wuce" mutuwa tsaye majeed yayi yana binta da k'allo kafadar shi ya d'aga tare da sakin killing smile yabi stair's din da k'allo ,wai gowa ya kuma yi ko zai ga yarinyar amma ba alamar mutun. sama ya hau direct part din sadiq ya wuce a hankali ya murda handle din kofar ya shigo sadiq na zaune saman three star yana kallan news cikin sallama ya qaraso amsa mai sallamar yayi tare da aika mai harara wasa, sai yau zaka nemeni dan raini senses? dariya majeed ya saki tare da cewa "Sorry " ya zauna ni dai yanzu ba wannan zancan ba wacece na gani yanzu kamar d'aga part din nan ta fito? gaban sadiq ne ya yanke ya fadi qaqaro murmushi k'arfin h'ali yayi yace "Fatuha ce" *(Ohni salma🤦‍♀ yaushe fatuha ta waye haka🙄lol)* ok majeed yace tare da cije lips dinsa  yana ayana  wasu abubuwa a ransa......hmmmm

           71_72

*_Ina matukar Godiya ga masoyan wannan novel, ina jin d'adin yadd'a kuke bani goyan b'aya wannan shafin naku ne duk masoyin CAPTAIN SADIQ._*

  

Ko da fatuha ta shig'a bedroom wayar tace ta hau ruri d'ake bedside drowe, cikin fara'a ta d'auka saboda sunan dak'e maqale a screen din wato hanan, daukar wayar tayi tare da kallan screen din kasancewar video call ne saman bed ta haye, "Hey yan'mata ya kike?" lafiya lau, wai yaushe zaki zo gida ne? ina so muje gidan ammi birthday din basma ya kusa, a ina za'ayi birthday din? inji fatuha kema kin san ba'a gida za'ayi ba, tap aikin san h'alin uncle wallahi ba ze barni naje ba, nifa matsala ta d'ake kenan wallahi, ke komai uncle komai . Eh naji din, yanzu dai ki mai dubara sai muje , gaskiya ina tsoran fushin shin kin manta randa muka je birthday din haoula kenan, wallahi ban manta ba ina sane ras, toh yayi kyau , amma fa zan shigo insha Allah .to shikenan ki gaishe min da angel tam ta katse wayar tare da kwaciyar ta.


    Kamar yadd'a suka saba in suka h'adu haka suka rinqa zuba hira tare da yin musu, d'ama sun saba baza su taba rabuwa ba tare da sunyi musu ba, amma duk wannan hirar da su majeed suke tunanin  fatuha na maqale a ransa, kiran sallah magrib ne ya tashe suka d'auro alwala  suka wuce masallaci. basu suka dawo ba sai bayan sallah isha, ko da suka dawo majeed bai shiga gidan ba d'aga waje ya tsaya tare da jan motar shi yana tunanin ta inda zai bulo ma fatuha dan a gaskiya a shirye yake da ya aure ta kota halin kaka.......hmmm tofa🤭


    Ko da sadiq ya shigo gidan mom na zaune a parlour ita da angel suna kallo, kallan parlour sadiq yayi tare da cewa "Angel ina Anuty?" tana bedroom Ddy. ok yace sannan ya haura sama ko  minutes 5 bai yi ba ya dawo parlour tare da wuce wa bedroom din fatuha, zaune take saman bed tana assignment da laptop din ta sanye take da rigar bacci dai-dai gwiwa sai gashin k'anta data nanade shi k'amar ganmo, 

      Bud'e kofar da akayi ne yasa ta d'aga eye's ball's din ta ture laptop din tayi tana qoqarin tashi, harara sadiq ya aika mata, tare da cewa "Ina car Key's dina?" zuburo baki tayi gaba, wato kin rai nani ko? shine zan fita kika d'auke min car Key's kika boye . sorry uncle wallahi bana so ka fita ne ka sha rana, Kuma da ka fita ai da bakwan ka be same ka ba am sorry tasa hannuwata biyu a kunne Allah yaba ka  hakuri. matsowa sadiq yayi kusa da ita har tana jin saukar numfashin shi  ja mata baki yayi har sai da ta saki yar qara, tabe baki tayi tana shirin yin kuka kul kika min kuka wallahi yau sai kin kwana kina kneel down kuma kin san zan iya, bani Key's din, bedside drowe ta bude tare da zaro Key's din ta miqa mai tana qoqarin komawa saman bed  janyo hannu ta yayi yana qare mata kallo ba qaramin kyau ta mai ba, lumshe ido yayi tare da ware su a k'anta murmushi fatuha ta saki mai cike da sakwanni iri-iri, rufe idanuwan ki kiga wani abu cikin sauri da zakuwa ta lumshe idanuwan ta wani dan kit ya fido tare da bude kit din oya bud'e wata hadadiyar sarkar gold ce wani irin ihu fatuha tasaki   tare da rungume sadiq ,shima rungume ta yayi tare da kai hannu saman gashin ta yawarware daurin data mai. cikin cool voice din shi yace "Gashin ki yafi kyau a h'aka" smile tayi tare da dagowa d'aga fafadan qirjin shi ta juya baya tana facing din dressing mirror tare da matsar da gashin ta gefe samata sarkar sadiq yayi ba qaramin kyau sarkar ta mata ba juyowa tayi tare da cewa "Uncle na gode" murmushi sadiq yayi nike da Godiya fatuha yau kusan shekarana uku bana tunanin, bana zuwa asibiti .murmushi ta saki tare da fita d'aga d'akin tana Isa parlour ta fad'a jikin mom, mom kin ga sarkar da uncle ya siya min? murmushi mom tayi irin nasu na manya amma dai gaskiya mun gode wannan kyau ta haka, lokacin yayi dai-dai da fito warshi d'aga bedroom din fatuha, angel ce ta turo baki tace "Daddy ni fa☹?" ja mata kumatu yayi tare da fido mata zube na gold ya sa mata murmushi ta saki love u daddy tare da yimai pack, lalai abin ma yar h'aka ce kenan? yaran ka kawai ka Sani ni Kuma ko oho mom ta fada, bayan mom yaje tare da zuro kanshi ta wuyan ta sorry mom next time ke zan siyawa kin ji Dan smile tayi sannan shima ya zagayu ya zauna sun dade suna hira sannan kowa ya wuce bedroom din shi.


      Bangaran majeed ko cikin farin ciki ya koma gida, ko da umma ta tanbaye shi sai ce mata yayi ya kusa cika mata burin ta, murna ko har ba'a magana ko da ya koma d'akin shi da ya rintsa ido fatuha yake gani h'aka ya rinqa juyi saman bed har bacci barawo ya sace shi.


  *Asuba ta gari*

Kasan Cewar yau weekend fatuha ta shiga kitchen tare da su baba jinmai suna aikin suna zuba hira kunsan a tare suka gama komai ,da misalin karfe 10:30 take tsaye gaban dining tana jera kayan abincin  cikin natsuwa  sallamar da kayi ne yasata dagowa tare da kallan kofar ta amsa a taqaice majeed ne ,sanye da shada ash tasha aiki sai kamshi yake zubawa dining area yanufo tare da yin smile fararan hakoran sa suka bayana. gyara murya yayi tare da cewa "Dama ashe kyawawa na aiki, gaskiya kamata yayi ace warhaka kina barcci ?" dagowa fatuha ta danyi a tunanin ta tuntuni ya shige ciki dan tasan kowaye a gidan saboda tasha ganin pics nashi dan murmushi ta saki cikin yauki tace "Suwaye kyawawa anan? naga dai ni kadai ce  ta ida zancan tana wai gawa"  murmushi ya Kuma saki a qaro na biyu fatuha kenan duk inda wata kyakyawa take kin kai ai, idanma b'aki sani bâ to y au zan fada miki."wallahi kin h'ada d'uk wani abun da ake nema wajen wata y'a mace, yar dariya ta saki wallahi yau uncle da tsoka ka zo gidan nan ko?yau dai  ranar tsokana ce? b'ata ida rufe b'akin ta ba sadiq ya shigo sanye da kayan gym din shi da alama d'aga gym ya d'awo, tunda yaga fatuha da majeed tsaye gabaki daya anurin fuskar shi ya sauya kamar an aiko mai da mutuwa daurewa yayi ya qaqaro murmushi my man yaushe ka shigo? wallahi yanzu na shiga bama kowa parlour sai wannan kyakyawar d'ake ta zuba aiki, Ni fa ko kallan ta ma kake ya Isa ya kosar da mutun d'aga yunwa .wani irin kallo sadiq  ya wurga ma fatuha tare da bin kayan jikin ta da kallo gown ce mai kyau sai dai ya matseta duk wata sura na jikin ta ana gani, ganin kallan da sadiq ya mata yasa ta barin parlour ba shiri ,majeed Kuma ya bita da kallo har yana cewa har zaki gudu? ko tanka mai ba tayi ba ta shige bedroom ,wai gowa yayi yana kallan sadiq ko lura da irin canjin da fuskar shi yayi bai lura ba ya haura upstairs, sadiq ya biyo bayan shi bayan sadiq yayi wanka ya shirya cikin kanana k'aya d'aya saba sawa suka fito tare da majeed dining area suka nufa, lokacin kowa ya halara gaishe da su mom sukayi a tare, tare da jan kojera suka zauna, a'a yau wanake gani a gidan ?dan murmushi majeed yayi yana sosa keya lalai ka kyauta, amma dai wuni ka zo yi? eh mana ki sha kurimin ki mom nama kusa kwaso  kayana na dawo nan da zama ya ida zancan sa yana sunkuyar da kan sa kasa, dariya mom da abba sukayi sadiq ko ya cika fam kamar zai fashe. janyo plate fatuha tayi zatayi saving din sadiq kamar yadda ta saba, sai da ta gama samin nashi zatayi saving din majeed ya fakaici idanuwan su ya riqe hannu  fatuha alamar kar ta zuba bata dau abin komai ba a tunanin hutar da ita yake son yi, ta zauna ta fara cin abincin ta sadiq ne yayi saving din   majeed da kanshi sannan suka fara break duk wata loma da majeed zai kai sai ya kai kallan shi ga fatuha idan sukayi ido hudu ya aika mata killing smile din shi duk wannan abun a qwayar idanuwa sadiq, cikin bacin rai ya ture plate din shi cike da masifa da zafin rai ya bar dining,  kallo su M'y suka  bishi da shi, fatuha ce tayi qoqarin tashi mom tace "Kyale shi ki cigaba da cin abincin ki ,tunda kika ga h'aka ba qaramin bata mai rai a qayi ba d'aga kafada majeed yayi tare da cewa " Indai sadiq ne ai ba yau ya saba haka ba" tunda sadiq ya bar dining fatuha ta rasa natsuwar ta ,tunda safe ta lura yake d'acin rai ta rasa dalili...hmm!


    Ko da ya shiga part din shi a parlour ya zauna tare da lumshe ido wani irin bacin rai na damun shi ,Kuma  gashi ya rasa dalilin h'aka dafa shin da akayi ne yasashi ware eye's ball's din shi fatuha ce tsaye da plate a hannu ta cikin cool voice din tace "Uncle pls kayi break koma waye ya bata ma rai kayi hakuri ta ida zancan tare da zama tana miqa mika ma plate din" amsa yayi ya aje gefe yana kallan fatuha kamar me neman wani abu a fuskar ta can Kuma ya kauda kai tare da  cewa zanci kin ji tashi ki je bana san magana ne ya fadi yana lumshe ido fita ta zo yi lokacin yayi dai-dai da shigowar majeed  bigewa su kayi da junnan su, "ohshh majeed yace" haka ma fatuha tayi b'aya waigowa sadiq yayi yana kallan su wani miyau mai daci ya hadiya. dariya fatuha ta saki tare da cewa "Sorry , majeed" yace tunda nazo gidan nan kike bige ni, har saida kika bige zuciya ta. zaro ido waje fatuha tayi tana dan kule bakin ta tace "Kai uncle ba ruwana ta fadi zancan tana ida fita" ritse ido sadiq yayi tare da dunqule hannuwa shi yana mai da numfashi sama sama majeed ne ya dafa shi lumshe ido yayi a hankali yana janyo natsuwar shi zagayuwa yayi ya zauna , big man ina tunanin ka daina zafin ranan ashe har yanzu kana yi , wai ma waya bata ma rai haka big man?kawai am not in the mood shiyasa eyya sorry ko alura ce ta motso ya fadi yana dariyar shaqiyanci pillow kujera sadiq ya wurga wa majeed wallahi ka rainani kai naka bai tashi ba sai nawa, hhhh, kasan me ammi tace a'a tace wallahi next meeting in bamu fido mata ba wallahi auran dole zata mana. Ni dai nayi mata wallahi very soon zaku Santa ,amma kai ban sani ba kana nan kana ruwan idon nan naka, dan tab'e baki sadiq yayi tare da cewa "Ba wani ruwan ido har yanzu b'anga wadda ta d'ace d'ani bane shiyasa?ko za kayi bayani sanda ammi zata kwasoma yar kauye. Dariyar yake  sadiq yayi tare da daukar remote control...

Sai baya'n sallah isha majeed ya bar gidan su sadiq, bayan sadiq ya d'awo party din shi ya wuce yana shiga yaci qaro da angel na kallo cartoon, murmushi ya saki tare da cewa, "Angel? ya qaraso kusa da ita ya zauna pack ya mata a goshi" daddy yes dear please gobe  muje shan ice cream please, sweetheart gobe ba time kin ga akwai work amma inna dawo da wuri zamu je kijin angel ok daddy, daddy yau a nan zan kwana? ta ida zancan ta cikin shagwaba ." dan ja mata h'anci sadiq yayi tare da cewa "Yanzu kin zama big girlfa? ko baki gani yanzu fa u are 9 year old ko baki Sani baby?" smile tayi ta ce "Haka kuma nima daddy zan zama kamar Anuty fatuha ko idan na girma?" yes mana smile ta saki tare da yimai pack a goshi, good night daddy, night angel.



Fatuha ce tsaye g'aban dressing mirror tana feshe jikin ta da turare da alama shirin bacci  take, dan kayan bacci ne a jikin ta pink da ratsin green riga ce dai-dai gwiwa mai dan siririn hannu bayan ta kamala shirin ta, ta baza gashin ta har gadan bayanta.  tsurawa dressing mirror din ido tayi tana tuna lokaci da sadiq yasa mata sarka lumshi ido tayi tare da daurasu a wuyan ta saurin ware ido tayi tare da cewa "U are very stupid fatuha tare da fita d'aga daki'n" bedroom din angel ta shiga lokacin har ta shirya cikin night dress din ta, tana kwace cikin blanket a hankali fatuha qaraso kusa da ita ware ido angel tayi kasan cewa bacci be dauke ta ba shafa gashin kanta fatuha tayi tace "Angel bakiyi bacci ba?" um yanzu na d'awo d'aga party din daddy, ok pack fatuha tayi mata tare da gyara mata blanket din ta kashe haskyan daki'n tare da rage gudun AC dakin ta fita.


    Ko da fatuha ta fita kitchen ta shige ta had'a coffee  sannan ta daura cup din a dan qaramin plate na glass ta haura saman, a hankali tayi knocking din part din come in aka ce hakan yasa fatuha bude kofar a hankali ta shigo. sanyi AC ke tashi da kamshi na musanman cikin fara'a ta qarasa shigowa sadiq na zaune saman three star sanye da night dress din shi maroon wando da riga, cikin natsuwa ta samu guri ta zauna saman one star, miqa mai glass cup din tayi amsa yayi yana smile kurba biyu zuwa uku yayi tare da kallan fatuha yace kamar akwai magana a bakin ki dan murmushi tayi tana wasa da yatsun hannu ta, ina jin ki! uncle dama dama um gobe ina so naje family house ne ni da hanan wani irin miyau sadiq ya h'ade take nan coffee din ya fara fita ranshi ,waza taje gani family house ?ya fada cikin zuciyar sa  qara h'ada rai yayi kamar an aiko mai da saqwan mutuwa majeed ne ya fado mai a rai qara tsuke fuska yayi yace "Ban shirya ma zuwan ki family house ba" ya fadi a taqaice, uncle... d'aga mata hannu yayi yace "Yawon yayi yawa ina laifin kije gidan Anuty bilki, next time kin je can din ?ya fadi a taqaice dan murmushi tayi wanda iya karshi laban ta, saboda bata san gardama da sadiq ko dan karya bata rain shi, qoqarin fita ta take jiki ba qwari janyo ta sadiq yayi ta fado saman fafadan qirjin shi dariya fatuha ta saki tare da dan dukan qirjin shi cikin wasa, murmushi ya saki tare da d'ago da habarta yace " Na dauka kinyi fushi da uncle ne" dan bata fuska tayi tare da taba gefan fuskar shi tace ba " Ba Komai uncle,   Kuma dama can gidan Mami nayi niyar zuwa hanan ce tace na tanbaye ka muje can" qarewa lips din ta kallo sadiq yake har ta kai aya, sannan yace "Ok!" zame jikin ta d'aga na Sadiq tayi tace "Uncle bari na tafi" ok har kin fara jin bacci ne na dauka hira kika zo taya ni dan smile tayi wanda ya bayana fararan hakoran ta tace "Ba bacci nake ji ba kawai zan tafi ne saboda bana san dare yamin a nan ina jin tsora fita ni ka dai" d'aga gira sadiq yayi ok good night, har ta kai bakin kofa ta jiyu tace "Oncle zaka taya ni hira kafin bacci ya dauke ni?" yap ok zan kira ka video call ina je bedroom dawowa tayi ta mai pack a goshi sannan ta fice d'aga part din.

     Ko da ta koma bedroom laptop din ta ta kuna, a bangaran sadiq ma haka kiran fatuha ya shigo laptop din sadiq dauk'a yayi yana kallan ta kamar tana gaban shi surutu ta cigaba da zuba mai yana dariya har cikin shi na kulewa haka itama sosai sukayi hira inda sadiq yake jin bacin rain d'ake damun shi ya sauka har tayi bacci suna video call smile sadiq yayi ganin fatuha tayi bacci sannan shima ya kwanta, amma duk da haka ya kasa kashe laptop din kuma ya kasa kauda idanuwashi d'aga kanta yarasa dalilin da yasa inda yana tare da fatuha yake bata guri na musanman a zuciyar shi ita ka dai ce macan d'ake ganin murmushin shi fara'a shi da halin da wannan tunanin bacci ya dauke sadiq ba tare da ya kashe laptop din ba.


    *Asuba ta gari*

Addu'a cike a bakin ta tashi tana miqa d'auke, laptop din ta dake saman bed take qoqarin sadiq ta hango kwance yana bacci , murmushi tayi tana qare ma kyakyawar face din shi kallo hannu ta kai kan screen din laptop din can kuma ta janye hannu ta daukar laptop din tayi ta aje saman sofa yadda zata riqa hango shi duk abinda take, toilet ta shige ta watsa ruwa tare da dauro alwala  daure da towel ta fito ko da ta fito lokacin sadiq shima ya tashi dan ba alamar shi ta cikin laptop din bed ba kowa sama ,cikin Sauri ta shirya tare da gabatar da sallah sket din makarantar ta ta janyo tasa tana gama sawa ta zura rigata tana bale ma balin riga ta, ta hango sadiq tsaye shima yana bale nashi dariya suka saki a tare morning uncle, morning too dear kin rigani tashi na riga ki shiryawa baki da sauri dan turo baki fatuha tayi dama can ka tashi idan ba hhhhh, kwarankwatsi ban tashi ba fashe wa fatuha tayi da dariya saboda tasan tsokanar ta yake kashe laptop din yayi domin ta qarasa shirya wa.


    Kusan a tare suka fito lokacin angel har ta shirya cikin uniform din ta tana dining area ita da mom da abba cikn natsuwa suka qaraso, gaishe da su mom su kayi suka amsa a tare sannan suka ja kujera suka zauna saving din su fatuha tayi sannan suka fara break cikin jin dadi bayan sun kamala pack sukayi ma mom, abba ne yace lalai gidan nan ana gwada min banbanci jiya ina kallo mom dinku kukayi ma pack ni kuma ko oho ba komai zan rama dariya sukayi su duka tare da ba abba  hakuri kusan a tare suka fito mom ta rako su har bakin kofa yaran sadiq sai qamewa suke, shigewa motoci sukayi abba ya shige nashi suka bar harabar gidan.


   Sosai yau sun sha karatu har da test sai da sukayi na history sosai fatuha ke maida hankalin ita da kawayan ta, Nana da Zara sune kawayan ta na makaranta kuma yan'yan masu kudi ne masu ji da Wayewa da yan matanci,   fatuha ko tun tana jss2 ta zana JSSCE saboda qoqarin da hazaqa yanzu haka SS2 take hanan na SS3 Basma kuma ta gama, Nana ce ta kalle ta bayan uncle ya fita daga class tace "Niko fatuha ina wannan uncle din nan naki mai kyau?" kallan ta fatuha tayi tare da aika mata harara ina ruwan ki da uncle dina, laaa fatuha yaushe kika zama haka? inji Nana, Zara d'ake gafe tace "Karki ga laifin ta wallahi ai dani ce fatuha da tuni na canza zani wallahi, hmmm ku wai me kuke nufi ban fa gane wannan hausar taku fatuha ta fada, hehe Zara da Nana sukayi lokaci daya, wallahi rainin hankalin nan namin dadi serious, to bari kiji ki zauna nan kwado ya maki kafa wallahi dani na sami damar ki da tuni na dama wallahi inji nana, ku dai Baku da aikin yi ni ina da . uncle kuma   nifa a matsayin wana na d'auke shi ba komai ba Allahu Akbar bugu fatuha ta kaima Nana da Zara da littafin dake hannu ta.



Basma ce zaune ita da umma tana taya ta kwalema a d'akin ta, pic din sadiq ne ya fado hannu ta kai ta dauka ta kurawa pic din ido umma ce ta taba ta firgigit ta dawo d'aga duniyar tunani tare da qoqarin aje pic. din murmushi umma ta saki yar auta me ke damun kine kwana biyu naga duk kin rame kin zabge kamar mara lafiya hawayan d'ake maqale a idanuwan tane suka zubu tare da riqe hannu umma, tace " Umma na rasa me ke damuna tun zuwan  da yaya sadiq yayi kwanaki na rasa meke damuna wallahi umma😭 ina son yaya sadiq ta ida zancan tare da rushewa da kuka" lalashin ta umma ta shigayi haba auta me abin kuka? share mata hawaye umma tayi duk wata  mace mai lafiya dole taso sadiq saboda ya tare duk abinda mata ke so basma insha Allah zan taya ki da addu'a, idan sadiq alheri ne a gare ki Allah ya baki shi inko haka ta faru sai nafi kowa farin ciki da kinga sai a h'ada da naki dana majeed tunda shima jiya yake fada min ya samu wadda yake dai-dai tawa da ita , murmushi basma ta saki tare da fita d'aga dakin da gudu umma ta bita da kallo tayi murmushi irin nasu Na Manya.


Misalin karfe 12:30pm suka tashi dag'a makaranta kasan cewa yau friday angel na gefan ta suna jiran drive, hadadiyar motace baka ta faka gaban su fatuha kauda kai fatuha tayi kamar motar ba gaban su ta tsaya ba ,saboda glass din motar tinted ne ko na ciki ba'a gani zuge glass din akayi hakan yasa angel washe baki tare da cewa "Second daddy?" majeed sanye cikin uniform din shi na aiki murmushi ya saki bale murfin motar angel tayi kamar tana jira ta shige gaishe shi fatuha tayi a taqaice tare da cewa angel fito kin san driver ne zai zo daukar mu ta fada, murmushi majeed yayi yace "To ni zan d'auki kyankyawa yau na kai ta har gida dan tun dazu na kori driven" dan zaro ido waje fatuha tayi tace "Sokake uncle ya Mani fada KO?" ah uncle ba zai maki fada ba ai yasan ni zan dauke ku dan smile fatuha tayi wanda iyakar shi laban ta ta bude murfin motar ta shiga cikin natsuwa ya ja motar inda yana driven yana kallan fatuha ta cikin mirror .sai da ya zo dai dai b'akin geta yayi horn bala mai gadi ya bude mai geta parking yayi su fatuha suka fito tare da mai Godiya sai da suka shige cikin gida yayi ribas ya fita.


        Shiga office din majeed sadiq yayi amma wayam ba majeed dan tabe baki yayi tare da fitowa d'aga office din ,da khaleed ya ci karo wanda shigowar shi yanzu kenan cikin fara'a sadiq ya tarbe shi, "Ah wai har yanzu majeed be dawo ba?" oh kama san da fita kenan eh munyi waya nace mai zanzo shine yace min yaje daukar baby shin d'aga school, baby sadiq ya maimai ta wallahi bansan sanda majeed zaiyi hankali ba wallahi mtww yaja Khaleed suka wuce office din shin. 


     Bayan sunyi lunch fatuha ta shirya tsaf cikin lace din ta maroon anmata dinkin bubu da lace din sosai kayan sun fido ta ,dan kwalin ta ta daura shi hawan hawan daurin ya zauna kamar gwagwaro janbaki ta shafa maroon da yar hod'a kwalliya sosai ta zauna ta tsantsara veil din ta ta dauk'o white ta yafa masha Allah fatuha ba qaramin kyau ta zuba ba,

   Tana fitowa parlour ta sami mom da majeed sunata zuba hira yana sanye da blue shad'a tasha aiki, cikin zazakar muryata fatuha tace "Mom zan je gidan mami, to, shikenan ki gaishe min da su zasu ji, majeed ne yayi karaf yace " Tunda tafiya nima zanyi bari na aje ki a can?" to! fatuha tace tare da yin gaba majeed yabi bayan ta shida kanshi ya bude mata murfin mota ta zauna  sannan ya bude ma kanshi ya shiga,

       Ko 20 minutes basu dauka ba suka Isa gidan Anuty bilki, inda majeed keta janta da surutu har ta dan saki jiki da shi yana yin horn mai gadi ya bude ya shigo da motar tare da yin parking qoqarin fita d'aga motar take majeed yace "Baki ji ba, waigowa tayi sannan yace karfe nawa zaki koma gida?" da yama ta fada a taqaice. toh! shikenan zanzo daukar ki Godiya ta mai tare da shigewa cikin gidan.


     Da gudu hanan ta rugo ta rungume ta kai amma naji dad'i wallahi, gaishe da mami tayi dake zaune parlour tana kallo sannan hanan ta ja ta bedroom suka baje suna ta zuba hira cikin jin dadi kai ka ce yan'uwane yadda suke nuna ma juna so sai kusan yama likis majeed ya zo gidan Anuty bilki inda ta rinqa zuba mai shaqiyanci shida sadiq sun raina ta basu san zuwa gidan ta sai da dalili shi dai  Sosa keya kawai yake sannan suka bar gidan suka nufo gidan,


    Tunda sadiq ya kunno kai da motar sa driven na ja shi kuma yana b'aya, gyaran murya yayi yace "Wancan motar kamar ta majeed? yallabai ita ce ai dazu dana zo daukar fatuha, Hajiya tace min ai majeed ya kaita to qila sun dawo ne, wani qulolin bakin ciki ya tsayawa sadiq har yana jin ya qasa hadiye miyau kusan a tare suka shigo gidan sai da fatuha ta fito majeed shima ya fito murmushi fatuha ta saki tana ma majeed godiya, qoqarin bude kofar motar driver keyi yi sadiq yayi magana a tsawace karka bude ya fadi, shi kan shi driver  terere yayi da mamakin canji sadiq lokaci d'aya .zaunawa sukayi a motar sadiq na hango fatuha da majeed, har tayi gaba ta dawo ,oh na manta wayana a motar smile majeed yayi tare da bude motar ya curo wayar ya bata, tare da cewa gaskiya yadda na dako wayar nan ya kamata ki bani number ki dan murmushi tayi tace uncle number ta eh mana ya fada ko baza'a bani ba zan baka mana curo wayar shi yayi d'ake gaban aljihu ya bama fatuha ta rubuta mai number ta sannan ya shige mota ya bar harabar gidan.


     Yana fita sadiq ya bude murfin motar cikin zafi, shigar fatuha bedroom ke da wuya taji an banka do  kofar cikin zafin rai waigowa tayi a dan tsorace fisgota sadiq yayi tare da fizge wayar da  dake hannu ta yayi wurgi da ita ta bigi Bango ta dawo a tarwatse ware ido fatuha tayi tare da sakin kuka, manna ta sadiq yayi da b'ango ya koma mata kamar zaki idanuwashi sunyi jawur kamar garwashi cikin kakausar murya yace "Waya baki izini fita gidan nan bada driver ba?" kuka fatuha ta kuma saki ko bakin magana ta rasa ganin bata da niyar bashi amsa yasa sadiq sakinta tare nufar kofar fita ya banka  ta da karfi ya fita hata mom dake part din ta sai da taji bugun kofar da sadiq yayi. zame wa fatuha tayi awurin tare da sakin kuka, koda sadiq ya Shiga part nashi cili yayi da bribriefcase din shi saman three star ya zauna tare da lumshe ido yana maida numfashe sama sama. ware idanuwan shi yayi saboda d'aya rintse fatuha da majeed ya yake gani shafa ganshin kanshi yayi da hannu yace noooooooo! Cikin d'aga murya........
